- **unlabeled-feature-linking-across-replicates**: Use when you have feature maps detected separately in multiple LC-MS runs (e.g., technical replicates or a dilution/spike-in series) and need a consensus feature map to extract per-run intensities for label-free quantification and reproducibility analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/unlabeled-feature-linking-across-replicates))
- **unlabeled-sample-based-correction**: Use when correcting LC-MS isotope-labeling FAM data from a high-resolution analyzer (e.g., Orbitrap at 140,000/280,000 nominal resolution) and an unlabeled-sample spectrum is available, especially for high-mass metabolites where resolution effects are strong. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/unlabeled-sample-based-correction))
- **unlabeled-sample-correction-matrix-estimation**: Use when correcting LC-MS isotope labeling data on high-resolving-power instruments (e.g. Orbitrap) where an unlabeled sample standard for each compound is available and the correction matrix must reflect the instrument's nominal resolution. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/unlabeled-sample-correction-matrix-estimation))
- **unlabeling-rate-quantification**: Use when benchmarking automatic cell-type identification methods on labeled scRNA-seq test data and you need to expose methods that abstain too often, complementing accuracy and F1-score on cross-platform or cross-validation splits. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/unlabeling-rate-quantification))
- **unlaunch**: Unlaunch integration. Manage data, records, and automate workflows. Use when the user wants to interact with Unlaunch data. ([membranedev/application-skills](https://github.com/membranedev/application-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/unlaunch) · ★273 · 3 copies)
